## *the* at pass 3, v389, each line whole before

**Each arrival stands here whole in the fields' own words, the incoming side: a hard problem as the scientific method poses it.** The same arrivals are carried resolved at the momentarying side, living and non-living existing things co-sequencing, prior, now and next, each resolving at the field's own next. **One learning at two sides.**

The first is required by replication, the second by measurement, the third by the inference, the fourth by replication. The fifth is the condition on what meets the statement, and the first four stand on it: a statement holds across readings where nothing arriving at it brings a prior reading.

The five fix what enters and how it executes. A problem adds a sixth, its own, fixing what comes out: the outcome preserves a stated relation over a stated range. This condition is the problem's and not the method's, and it stands at Conserving relation and Conserving reach in every entry.

**The problems stand open.** No entry carries a settled answer to the statement it holds, except where the record settles one and the entry carries that settling.

**The properties and the checks stand open.** Every property is stated at section 4 and every check at section 6. Any of them is executed against the fields' literature, by anyone holding it.

**Every line stands open at an address.** A filled property keys to the place its statement is verified. A line without an address makes a claim that cannot be met, and check C1 tests for it.

Against these, one thing stands fixed: the properties themselves. Every entry carries the same properties, in the same order, whatever the field. Uniform elements are what make two entries comparable, and comparability is what a registry is for. The schema is closed once, at one place, so that nothing else has to be.

**Problems in science** are hypotheses. A hypothesis is an account with consequences following from it, tested together with the specifications it carries. Where a consequence fails, the conjunction of account and specifications is refuted, and no member of it is identified. Where a consequence holds, nothing follows about the account, a rival account entailing the same consequence.

**Hard** is where testing continues and returns no refutation: either no consequence separating the accounts is derivable, or the accounts entail the same consequences. Both are properties of the hypothesis together with what it carries, and neither is a property of what the hypothesis is about.

**Persistence** is the remainder between what stands and a settling result.

Each entry states one problem as it is posed, and carries progress and persistence with the remainder between them visible. Every entry carries the same properties, filled from the record at which each is verified.

A refutation falls on the conjunction of account and specifications. No member of it is identified. A test therefore returns no indication of which specification is at issue where a settling result requires one not to hold.

Accumulation stands in the specifications. A result that stands becomes a specification of tests that follow. The inference reads the specifications at the start of a test and writes to them at no point.

Where a problem is durable, the specifications stand fixed across the tests run against them. The number of hypotheses tested measures the testing.

Refinement sharpens the statement, the accounts, or the instruments, within the specifications standing.

Revision withdraws a specification, retains the accounts, and tests again. The inference licenses neither, having refuted a conjunction and identified no member.

Both operate on what the specifications permit. A settling result the specifications do not permit is reached by neither.

An entry carries two parts. The properties are of the problem, and stand whether or not work has been done on it. The report is of work done, and stands empty where none is recorded.

Each states the record at its address, or stands empty.

**Conditions prior.** The five, stated in every entry. Every unreachability result derives from these together with the specifications below.

**Statement.** The form in which the problem is posed, in the words it is posed in. Variant formulations stand, unranked.

**The field's account of its hardness.** Where a field states, in its own words, what is hard about its own problem — before any account is proposed. Stated from the field's review or methodological literature, or empty. No inference from the difficulty of the record.

**Accounts.** What each account proposes to exist, on its own content, at the strength its record carries. The number is as posed. No relation between accounts stands here. A statement about the condition of the effort proposes no account.

**Specifications.** Each specification the statement carries beyond the five, with the name it is universal under, or local. The number stands against the number of consequences derivable.

**Specifications withdrawn.** Each specification the record carries as given up with the accounts retained. No reason stands, a refutation identifying no member.

**Common requirement.** What the accounts require alike, where stated.

**Conserving relation.** What the outcome is required to preserve, stated as the problem states it: an ordering that does not cycle, a procedure that returns, a predicate that divides cases, a value standing prior to measurement.

**Conserving reach.** The range over which the relation is required, stated as the problem states it: all profiles, all programs, all statements of the language, all cases.

**Unreachability.** A result establishing that the conserving relation is not preserved over the conserving reach. The result is about the conditions and stands whether or not an attempt was made. What was attempted stands at Approaches.

**Persistence.** The settling result specified.

**Addresses.** The location at which each property is verified, keyed property by property.

**Approaches.** Each direction taken, with its present reach in the measures used, and its standing: refuted; blocked by a proved limitation; equivalent by construction to a rival; answering an adjacent question by the method's own account; instruments disagreeing on the object; narrowing; open.

**Narrowing recorded.** Where the record carries the reach narrowed, or the relation weakened, with results following. Each is stated as the record states it.

**Own record.** Where a statement about the problem's own standing is made, phrased as it is made.

No person functioning as attribution, and no date. Eponyms that are technical vocabulary stand where that vocabulary puts them. Years stand at the addresses.

A precise statement carries nothing the original statement together with its own specifications does not carry.

Priority and sequence. Contested wherever recorded, and no standing rests on it. Names and years stand at the addresses, locating a text.

The five stand in every entry as they stand at the front, the problem being what was fixed in order for the method to act on it.

**A property standing empty is an observing and not a gap.** Nothing recorded at that property, and the entry carries that; the record carrying it and the entry not, and check C1 through C19 finds it. **An emptiness is recorded at the entry and not at the intake.**

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness, accounts, approaches: Hyde and Raffman, "Sorites Paradox", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy, substantive revision 26 August 2025, §§2–3.4. **Statement.** Neurons signal with discrete spikes. Is the information carried by how many spikes occur, or by when they occur? In the field's own analysis, the information in a population's spike times separates into the information in the spike counts and additional terms contributed by timing, and the question is whether, over the observation window at issue, the timing terms carry information beyond the counts.

**Persistence.** The used variable identified downstream rather than the information available upstream, generalised beyond the systems where timing manipulation has behavioural effects.

**Accounts.** No: a conclusion containing an evaluative term cannot be validly derived from premises containing none, since validity preserves only what the premises contain. The point is logical rather than substantive, holds whatever the facts turn out to be, and is what makes moral criticism of any factual arrangement possible at all — if ought followed from is, whatever is the case would be what ought to be.

The gap is bridged by the meanings of terms: institutional and functional facts carry evaluative content, and inferences from them to obligations are ordinary. The prohibition assumes a separation of vocabularies language does not observe.

**Specifications.** Statements sort into descriptive and evaluative — local. Validity is a relation between premises and conclusion preserving content in the premises — universal under logic.

Formalising the prohibition — proofs existing for restricted logical languages, their scope being exactly the restriction.

**Statement.** Do smooth solutions of the equations of fluid motion in three dimensions remain smooth for all time, or can they develop a singularity? And the turbulence problem, also at the title, posed by the field on its own: whether a predictive theory of turbulent flow follows from the equations, the averaged equations for the mean flow carrying more unknowns than equations at every order — the closure problem.

**Accounts.** They remain smooth: no singularity has been observed in any physical fluid or reliable simulation, and the equations describe fluids accurately across the range where they are tested.

Not established, and blow-up is plausible: the scaling of the nonlinear term against dissipation is supercritical in three dimensions, which is exactly the regime where the available methods fail, two dimensions being settled precisely because it is not supercritical.

**Common requirement.** That the equations continue to describe the fluid at every scale, so that a question about all time and all initial data is well posed.

**Unreachability.** An averaged version of the equations admits blow-up, showing that any proof of regularity must use structure the averaged version discards, so a class of arguments is proved unable to reach it. The holding is not reached over all initial data by methods that do not use that structure.

**Persistence.** A proof of global regularity, or a constructed solution developing a singularity. For the closure problem, a closure derived from the equations rather than supplied by a model.

**Statement.** Two particles prepared together are measured far apart. The results are correlated more strongly than any account giving each particle its own properties allows. What produces the correlation?

The theorem constrains a class of theories, and which assumption to give up is not settled: nonlocality, denial of measurement independence, retrocausality, and superdeterminism each preserve the correlations at a different cost, and the field has no agreed criterion for choosing among them.

**Common requirement.** That the correlation is produced by something specifiable at one side or transmitted between the sides.

**Conserving reach.** All accounts preserving the observed correlations.

**Unreachability.** The surviving accounts are empirically equivalent to each other, each preserving the correlations at a different cost, and the field carries no agreed criterion for choosing among them. The account is not reached over the surviving class by the coincidence counts.

**Approaches.** Closing the loopholes — detection, locality, and freedom-of-choice loopholes closed, the last using cosmic photons and human random choices, the correlation standing and the interpretation not following. Local hidden variables — excluded by the theorem plus experiment, the route closed outright. Nonlocal hidden variables — reproduce everything, explicitly nonlocal, empirically equivalent. Superdeterminism — preserves locality by denying setting independence, not excluded by any experiment, its critics holding it undermines the practice of experiment. Retrocausal accounts — consistent constructions existing, no distinguishing prediction so far. Information-theoretic reconstructions — derive the correlations from principles such as information causality and reach the observed bound, accounting for the magnitude without settling what produces it.

**Statement.** Whether a quantum entity is a wave, a particle, both, or neither, given that it shows interference in one experimental arrangement and localized detection in another — and whether the question is well-posed, the field's standing answer, complementarity, holding the two descriptions mutually exclusive and jointly required, and the delayed-choice arrangements leaving what that answer establishes in dispute.

**Accounts.** Neither, and the statement is malformed: the formalism is complete, wave and particle are classical pictures, and the two descriptions apply to mutually exclusive experimental arrangements.

**Specifications.** An entity has a determinate nature specifiable independently of the measurement made on it — local. The two classical pictures exhaust the available descriptions — local.

**Unreachability.** Obtaining path information destroys interference, quantitatively, by measured duality relations, which establishes the trade exactly without settling the nature; and the interpretations are empirically equivalent by construction. The fact is not reached over the arrangements by preparation settings and detection events.

**Persistence.** A determination of the entity's nature between preparation and detection, which the empirical equivalence of the interpretations excludes.

**Approaches.** Which-path measurement — obtaining path information destroys interference, quantitatively, by measured duality relations, establishing the trade exactly. Delayed choice — the experiment performed, the outcome matching quantum prediction under every arrangement, the interpretation disputed. Quantum eraser — interference recoverable by correlation with the marker, widely misreported as retrocausal, the field stating the correlations require the joint measurement. Weak measurement of trajectories — reconstructs average trajectories in a two-slit apparatus, what an average trajectory establishes about individual entities being disputed. Interpretation — each giving its own answer, and they are empirically equivalent.

The programme survives in modified form: the consistency of arithmetic is proved by transfinite induction to a specified ordinal, and reverse mathematics has established exactly which axioms are needed for which theorems. What was lost is the finitary requirement, not the enterprise.

**Conserving relation.** A system capturing the arithmetical truths and proving its own consistency.

**Unreachability.** Each strengthening carries its own unprovable sentence, by construction at every stage, and consistency is unprovable within the system. The capture is not reached over the arithmetical truths by derivation in any system strong enough for arithmetic.

**Persistence.** Within the conditions, nothing: the results are proved. Outside them, which axioms suffice for which theorems, settled case by case.

**Approaches.** Strengthening the system — each strengthening carrying its own unprovable sentence, by construction at every stage. The transfinite consistency proof — succeeds, using induction to an ordinal not available within arithmetic, requiring at a higher level what it was meant to secure at a lower. Reverse mathematics — maps exactly which axioms suffice for which theorems, with striking regularity across five subsystems, a substantial positive result addressing a different question. Ordinal analysis — extends the transfinite route upward, each system's proof-theoretic ordinal established, each needing a stronger metatheory. Set-theoretic realism — holds truth outruns provability and the unprovable sentences are true, consistent, and supplying no method.

**Narrowing recorded.** The transfinite consistency proof weakens the relation: consistency stands proved by means stronger than the system rather than weaker. Reverse mathematics narrows the reach: which axioms suffice stands theorem by theorem rather than for the truths as a whole.

**Statement.** Is there a set larger than the integers and smaller than the real numbers?

**Accounts.** The statement has no answer: the hypothesis is consistent with the standard axioms and so is its negation. It is independent, and independence is the answer.

Independence from one axiom set is not the end: the axioms are incomplete, and the question is which further axioms are true. Programmes on inner models and on forcing axioms aim at settling it, and some favour a definite value.

**Specifications.** The sets form a determinate universe about which questions have answers — local. The standard axioms describe that universe — local.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of the hypothesis, standing on the axioms.

**Conserving reach.** All models of the standard axioms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether a further axiom is true of the sets, beyond being consistent with them. Consequences of chosen axiom systems are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Independence is proved twice over, and large-cardinal axioms provably do not settle it, forcing preserving them. The determination is not reached over the models by the standard axioms or by the large-cardinal extensions.

**Persistence.** A criterion for the truth of a further axiom, beyond its consistency.

**Conserving relation.** An analysis reproducing the sorting, the reproduction standing over cases not yet constructed.

**Statement.** Any claim can be asked for its grounds. The grounds can be asked in turn. Where does this end? In the field's own statement the demand for a foundation must end at one of three horns, verbatim: "infinite regress, circularity, or that kind of dogmatism that is seen as a necessary evil", the third a solution one resigns oneself to accepting in view of the obvious unserviceability of the other two.

**Common requirement.** That justification terminates, circles, or continues, and that these three exhaust the options.

**Statement.** Whether the motion of three bodies under their mutual gravitation admits a general solution. In the classical sense — an expression in known functions giving the positions at any later time — the field holds the question settled in the negative: no algebraic integrals exist beyond the classical ten, and the convergent series that does exist converges too slowly for use. **Approaches.** Peripheral blockade — relieves some patients and not others, read by both accounts as supporting theirs for those patients. Skin biopsy for small-fibre density — moves a measured fraction of patients into the peripheral category. Functional imaging — identifies central changes reliably, whether cause or consequence not settled by the imaging, which the field states. Clinical assessment of sensitization features — quantitative sensory testing and symptom-based instruments, assessing the features rather than the mechanism, which the field distinguishes explicitly. The third mechanistic descriptor — introduced as a pain phenotype alongside the nociceptive and neuropathic, defined by absence of clear evidence for either, and applied residually where the other criteria are not fully met. The multifactorial model — peripheral drivers and central mechanisms held to combine in the same condition, with osteoarthritis recorded as the worked case, and the field stating that the presence of central features should not stop the search for peripheral dysfunction. Prognostic use — sensitization features associated with poorer outcomes and greater risk of persistent pain after peripherally focused interventions, used to direct referral rather than to identify the driver. Persistence after structural removal — pain continuing after joint replacement where the implicated structures are no longer present, recorded as evidence for a central contribution. Non-neuronal mechanisms — glial and immune contributions to sensitization at both sites, a distinct mechanistic route. Psychological and multidisciplinary treatment — improves outcomes substantially without identifying a driver. Channel-targeted and growth-factor drugs — efficacy in specific conditions, defining subgroups rather than settling the general question.

Its consequences include results widely regarded as unacceptable: a solid ball decomposes into finitely many pieces reassembling into two balls of the same size. The pieces are non-measurable, so no contradiction follows, and constructive mathematics proceeds without the axiom at known cost.

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which axioms hold of the sets.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the axiom holds of the sets, standing on the other axioms.

**Unreachability.** Independence is proved from both directions; and the dispute reduces to whether non-measurable sets are acceptable, which is the original dispute. The determination is not reached over the models by consequences of chosen systems.

**Persistence.** A criterion for the truth of the axiom beyond its consistency, or agreement on whether non-measurable sets are acceptable.

**Approaches.** Proving or refuting from the other axioms — consistency and independence both proved, closed by proof. Weaker choice principles — countable choice, dependent choice, the ultrafilter lemma, each sufficing for part of the practice and not all, the hierarchy mapped precisely. Determinacy axioms — contradict full choice, imply all sets of reals are measurable, and remove the decomposition, consistent relative to large cardinals, the conflict with choice being the trade. Constructive mathematics — proceeds without the axiom, the obtainable results precisely characterised, a known and exact cost. Locating the fault in measurability — the decomposition using non-measurable sets, whose existence follows from the axiom, the dispute becoming whether such sets are acceptable.

**Narrowing recorded.** Weaker choice principles narrow the reach: each stands for the part of the practice it suffices for, the hierarchy mapped precisely. Constructive mathematics narrows the reach: the obtainable results stand characterised without the axiom.

**Accounts.** There is no paradox: uncountability is a statement about the absence of a bijection, and the required bijection exists outside the model but not inside it, countability being relative to a model.

The relativity is the difficulty, not its removal: if "uncountable" holds relative to a model, then either there is an absolute notion the models approximate, or set-theoretic language does not determine its own subject matter.

**Specifications.** First-order axioms characterise the structures they are about — local. Cardinality is a property of a set — local.

**Common requirement.** That the axioms are about something, and the question is whether they determine it.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of the subject matter by the axioms, standing across the models.

**Conserving reach.** All models satisfying the axioms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the axioms fix their subject matter. Models satisfying the axioms, of which there are many non-isomorphic ones, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Relativisation removes the contradiction and both accounts accept it, the disagreement being about what follows; and second-order categoricity presupposes the set theory in question. The determination is not reached over the models by the models themselves.

**Persistence.** A characterisation of the subject matter not presupposing it, or agreement on what follows from the relativity.

Entrenchment describes practice without justifying it: the grue predicate is definable from green and a time, and green is equally definable from grue and bleen, so the asymmetry is not in the logic, and every proposed non-circular criterion of naturalness has been contested.

The criterion is not reached over the predicates by observations describable in either vocabulary.

Hardness: the encyclopedia's entry on the problem of induction, at passages of its text on the grue paradox as its archived editions carry them verbatim (the paradox making clear that something is wrong with instance confirmation and enumerative induction as initially characterized; no one taking the grue hypothesis well supported; neither the grue evidence statements nor the hypothesis entailing that any emerald changes colour; the paradox not resolvable by looking to background knowledge, its unlikelihood merely restating the point; the time parameter sometimes advanced as a criticism of the definition; the account of projectibility, roughly, that projectible hypotheses are made up of predicates with a history of use in projections), its author not carried at the returns, and the Internet Encyclopedia of Philosophy's entry on confirmation and induction as its page carries it (similar examples plentiful and a purely syntactical definition of confirmation commonly agreed not to do; projectibility by entrenchment coming very close to saying the projectible predicates are the ones in fact projected), its author not carried at the return, both returned in the search, neither fetched; the survey at the address not returned at its passages on the riddle, and the founding chapter, not observed this pass. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the induction entry's passages on the paradox observed at pass two (something wrong with instance confirmation and enumerative induction as stated; the projectible predicates to be parted from the others on some ground). Approaches: the entrenchment and naturalness routes at those passages and at the founding reference at the address. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Progress.** The interdefinability result. The entrenchment record. The mapped costs of each proposed criterion.

**Accounts.** Yes, by a negligible amount: the confirmation is real and tiny, proportional to the relative sizes of the classes, and the appearance of absurdity is a failure of intuition about small quantities.

The calculation depends on assumptions about the sampling and the class sizes that are supplied rather than derived, and reverses under different but equally reasonable assumptions: the relation between a generalisation and its instances is not settled by the calculation.

**Unreachability.** The calculation reverses under equally reasonable assumptions, and whether the shoe was drawn from non-black things or from shoes changes the result, which establishes that the relation depends on how evidence is gathered, not stated in the original statement. The determinacy is not reached over the relation by calculations under supplied assumptions.

**Addresses.** Statement: Hempel, "Studies in the Logic of Confirmation", Mind 54:1-26 and 97-121, 1945. The survey: Crupi, "Confirmation", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy; the Bayesian treatments: Fitelson and Hawthorne, "How Bayesian confirmation theory handles the paradox of the ravens", in The Place of Probability in Science, Springer, 2010, 247-275. Hardness: the survey at the address, at passages of its text as the encyclopedia's page, its archived editions and a mirror carry them verbatim (the paradox stated at a black raven and a non-black non-raven, one wanting to say the hypothesis is not confirmed by both alike and the satisfaction theory unable to draw the distinction; the hypothetico-deductive view blocking the derivation, the hypothesis not entailing that a generally sampled object will be a black raven, and reading the instance-confirmation insight differently, the evidence confirming relative to the auxiliary assumption that the object was taken among ravens; the view faring well enough with the paradox at the troubling conclusion that a white swan, or a million, does not confirm on it that a further swan will be white), the entry itself not fetched, its section numbers and revision date not carried at the returns; the founding paper and the Bayesian treatments not observed this pass. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the survey observed at pass two (the paradox stated at a black raven and a non-black non-raven; the Bayesian resolution's dependence on assumptions about the relative sizes of the classes), a confirmation relation not depending on assumptions supplied from outside standing there as what the survey records each resolution as lacking. Approaches: the Bayesian and equivalence-denying routes at the same survey's sections, observed at pass two. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Bayesian quantification — yields tiny confirmation under standard assumptions, the assumptions being the disputed point, reversals exhibited in the literature. Denying equivalence-invariance — blocks the paradox and gives up a condition most accounts regard as basic. Sampling considerations — establish that the relation depends on how evidence is gathered, which is not in the original statement. Selective confirmation — restricts confirmation to instances of the subject class, ad hoc by its critics' account, and blocks the paradox. Rejecting the confirmation-relation framework — taken by some, the alternative accounts carrying their own open problems.

**Progress.** The quantified confirmation under standard assumptions. The exhibited reversals. The demonstrated dependence on sampling.

**Statement.** What makes a person at one time the same person as a person at another time — the persistence question — and what it is for that relation to hold across gradual replacement of parts, loss and recovery of memory, and the imagined cases of division. Variant formulation, as posed of any thing: a thing has all its parts replaced gradually; is it the same thing? — the question the field carries separately as material constitution.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a relation across time. States at times, and the relations between them, are what is measured, all compatible with both criteria. The two are not the same.

**Addresses.** Statement: Olson, "Personal Identity", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy. The division cases: Parfit, Reasons and Persons, Oxford, 1984, Part Three. Material constitution as the field parts it: Wasserman, "Material Constitution", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy. Hardness: the same entry, at passages of its text as the encyclopedia's page and its archived editions carry them verbatim (reactions to the brain-transplant case possibly supporting only that psychological continuity is what matters practically, compatible with other accounts, leaving it open whether there is any reason to accept the psychological approach; fission sometimes said to afflict all answers to the persistence question equally, apart perhaps from anticriterialism, whether it does a hard question, and especially worrying for the psychological approach as threatening its support without affecting rivals; the non-branching view suggesting the practical concerns one has for oneself can apply to someone else; psychological-continuity views appearing to rule out our being biological organisms, no sort of psychological continuity necessary or sufficient for a human organism to persist), the entry itself not fetched, its section numbers and revision date not carried at the returns, the earlier archived editions carrying the Simple View where the later carry anticriterialism; the division cases and the constitution entry not observed this pass. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Progress.** The fission result against simple versions of both criteria. The mapped costs of each response.

Claimed cases dissolve on inspection: re-examination of the standard examples found the alleged superior alternatives not superior on the relevant margins, and no case of remediable inefficiency demonstrated.

**Unreachability.** The counterfactual performance at scale is unobservable by construction, and the historical cases were re-examined and the re-examinations contested in turn, which is the field's instruments disagreeing on the same episodes. The comparison is not reached over the cases by the adopted path or by small-scale comparison.

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness, and the increasing-returns mechanism at Accounts: Arthur, "Competing Technologies, Increasing Returns, and Lock-In by Historical Events", The Economic Journal 99(394):116-131, 1989, at its opening as the field's pages carry it verbatim, the paper itself not fetched. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding paper's opening observed at pass one and at the hardness (competing technologies under increasing returns locked in by historical events, the outcome possibly inefficient and not predictable from the technologies' merits), a remediable inefficiency demonstrated at scale standing there as the empirical question the paper leaves. Approaches: the formal-modelling route at that paper; the historical-case route at the references at the address, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Formal modelling — produces lock-in under stated conditions, whether the conditions obtain being the empirical question. Historical case studies — the standard cases re-examined and contested, the re-examinations contested in turn, no case agreed. Laboratory experiments — produce lock-in reliably under controlled network effects, transfer to markets with entry and side payments disputed. Looking for remediable inefficiency — requires the counterfactual performance at scale, unobservable by construction. Ex post comparison after a switch — comparison available where switches occur, selection into switching being the confounding, which the field notes.

**Progress.** The formal models and their stated conditions. The laboratory results under controlled network effects. The contested re-examination record.

**Statement.** Understanding a part of a text requires understanding the whole; understanding the whole requires understanding the parts. How does interpretation begin? In the field's own statement, the circle's classical formulation is that interpretive experience involves a circular relation of whole and parts: a text must be understood as a whole to understand any of its parts, and understood in each of its parts to be understood as a whole; the foundational law of understanding, in the formulation the field records as the first, is to find the spirit of the whole through the individual and to grasp the individual through the whole.

**Common requirement.** That there is something the interpretation is of, against which readings can be better or worse.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion for the correctness of a reading, standing outside the readings it adjudicates.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a criterion for the correctness of a reading. Readings, each already inside the circle, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Intention is reconstructed by interpretation and context is a text requiring interpretation, so each proposed criterion is itself inside the circle; and community-of-interpreters accounts give up the independent criterion, which their proponents accept. The criterion is not reached over the readings by readings taken from inside.

**Persistence.** A criterion standing outside the readings it adjudicates, or agreement that no independent criterion is owed.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: the entry "Hermeneutics", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy, at its account of the hermeneutical circle as the encyclopedia's current page carries it verbatim and at the passage on the circle's history as the encyclopedia's archived editions carry it verbatim, the entry itself not fetched, its author and revision date keyed at the next pass; the first formulation as the entry cites it: Ast, 1808, p. 178; the principle as the entry cites it: Schleiermacher, lecture of 1829. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the same entry, at its account of the circle observed at pass one and at the hardness (the understanding of the whole depending on the parts and of the parts on the whole; the circle's history from a method to a condition of understanding), a criterion standing outside the readings standing there as what the circle denies. Approaches: the intention and context criteria at the same entry's sections on the debate, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Authorial intention as the criterion — faces the difficulty that intention is itself reconstructed by interpretation. Historical context as the criterion — context being a text requiring interpretation, at one remove. Community of interpreters — validity being what the community accepts, accounting for practice and giving up the independent criterion, which its proponents accept. Philological method — establishes what the words meant at the time, with real results, underdetermining the readings in dispute, which are usually about significance rather than lexis. Rejecting the whole-part framing — reframes, the dependence reappearing at whatever units are chosen.

**Narrowing recorded.** Philological method narrows the reach: the criterion stands for questions of lexis rather than for the readings in dispute. Community-of-interpreters accounts weaken the relation: validity stands as community acceptance rather than as an independent criterion.

**Statement.** Some legal cases are not settled by the applicable rules. Does the law contain an answer, or does the judge make one? In the field's own posing, a hard case is one that cannot be resolved by an unequivocal legal rule set out by the appropriate body before the event, where on the positivist theory the judge then has a discretion to decide the case either way; the thesis posed against that theory holds that the judge in such a case does not make law but is bound by standards the law already contains.

**Accounts.** The law contains an answer: beyond rules there are principles with weight, and the correct answer is the one fitting and justifying the legal materials as a whole, judges seeking that fit rather than exercising discretion.

Where rules run out the judge exercises discretion and makes new law: legal materials are open-textured, they underdetermine outcomes in exactly the hard cases, and disagreement among competent judges over the same materials is the evidence.

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about whether the materials determine the case.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the materials fix the outcome, standing separately from the outcome reached.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the materials determine an outcome. Decided cases, in which an outcome was reached either way, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating whether the materials fixed the outcome from the fact that an outcome was reached.

**Addresses.** Statement: Dworkin, "Hard Cases", Harvard Law Review 88:1057, 1975, at its definition of a hard case as the field's pages carry the sentence verbatim, the paper itself not fetched. Hardness: the working paper on the Hart-Dworkin debate, Public Law and Legal Theory Working Paper Series no. 77, at its opening as the repository copy carries it verbatim, its author keyed at the next pass. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the working paper's opening observed at pass one and at the hardness (the debate whether the law runs out in hard cases and judges make it, or the materials fix the answer), a measurement separating the fixing from the reaching standing there as what neither side has produced. Approaches: the outcome-pointing and reasoning-analysis routes at that paper's account of the two positions. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

Not entirely: a rule of sufficient injustice fails as law, and legal systems have an internal morality without which they do not function as legal systems at all, the post-war tribunals and the treatment of enacted atrocity standing as the cases.

**Conserving reach.** All rules the question is asked of, the enacted atrocities included.

**Unreachability.** The grudge informer exchange produced two coherent treatments of one set of events reaching different characterisations, and comparative study documents both patterns extensively, supporting both readings. The sorting is not preserved across the cases by legal practice.

**Persistence.** A case sorting differently under the two accounts and agreed to have been sorted correctly by one.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: the entry "Legal Positivism", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy, at its opening and at its statement of the positivist thesis as the encyclopedia's current page and archived editions carry them verbatim, the entry itself not fetched, its author and revision date keyed at the next pass; the canonical sentence, which the entry attributes to Austin, as the entry cites it: 1832, p. 157; the natural-law side's own posing not observed this sitting and keyed at the next pass. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the same entry, at its opening and its statement of the thesis observed at pass one and at the hardness (the existence and content of law depending on social facts and not on merits; the natural-law denial), a case sorting differently under the two accounts standing there as what the thesis's test would be. Approaches: the grudge-informer exchange and the rule-of-recognition route at the same entry's sections and the references at the address, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Unreachability.** The erasure bound and the area scaling are established and read both ways, and the reconstruction axioms quantify over systems, which is the standing objection. The dependence is not established over the systems by measurements describable in either description.

**Persistence.** A distinguishing prediction between the two readings, which no informational ontology has produced.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Wheeler, "Information, Physics, Quantum: The Search for Links", in Proceedings of the 3rd International Symposium on Foundations of Quantum Mechanics in the Light of New Technology, Physical Society of Japan, Tokyo, 1990, 354-368, reprinted in Zurek (ed.), Complexity, Entropy and the Physics of Information, Addison-Wesley, 1990, at its abstract as the repository's page carries it verbatim and at its section 19.2 as the field's pages carry the passage verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the repository records the proceedings paper at pages 354-358 and 1989, the field's citations at pages 354-368 and 1990, each keyed as it carries it. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding essay observed at pass one and at the hardness (the search for links between information, physics and quantum theory; every physical quantity deriving its ultimate significance from yes-no questions), a distinguishing prediction standing there as what the essay proposes to seek and no informational ontology has produced. Approaches: the erasure-principle and black-hole-thermodynamics routes at the references at the address, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** The erasure principle — verified experimentally at the predicted bound, establishing a link and read both ways. Black hole thermodynamics — entropy scaling with area, a major result, its interpretation being the dispute at one more site. Information-theoretic reconstructions of quantum theory — derive the formalism from axioms about information processing, the axioms quantifying over systems, which is the standing objection. Informational ontology programmes — proposed, no distinguishing prediction produced. Digital physics and cellular automaton models — constrained by Lorentz invariance and by Bell correlations, specific proposals excluded.

**Narrowing recorded.** Information-theoretic reconstructions narrow the reach: the derivation stands from axioms about what can be done with systems rather than establishing the dependence.

**Accounts.** By partial knowledge: inquiry begins from knowing enough to specify the question and not enough to answer it, the two horns falsely assuming knowledge is all or nothing. A detective knows the crime and not the culprit, a chemist knows the reaction and not the mechanism, and in each case the object is specified under one description and sought under another, recognition being by the specifying description, which the inquirer has.

Specifying the question already presupposes the concepts the inquiry is meant to acquire, and the difficulty recurs at the level of concepts: acquiring a genuinely new concept cannot be described as recognising an answer to a question posed in prior terms.

**Conserving reach.** All acquisitions, the acquisition of primitives included.

**Unreachability.** Partial knowledge dissolves the classical version and does not reach the acquisition version; statistical learning accounts for acquisition from data with the architecture supplying the primitives, which is the objection; and the nativist argument stands unrefuted rather than accepted. The account is not reached over the acquisitions by learners already equipped.

**Persistence.** An account of primitive acquisition not presupposing the primitives, or a refutation of the argument that none is possible.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Ebrey, "Meno's Paradox in Context", British Journal for the History of Philosophy 22(1):4-24, 2014, at its abstract as the journal's and the repositories' pages carry it verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the reply on true belief and recollection: Fine, The Possibility of Inquiry: Meno's Paradox from Socrates to Sextus, Oxford University Press, 2014, as the field's reviews carry its reading (Bryn Mawr Classical Review 2015.10.03; Notre Dame Philosophical Reviews, review of the book, its date not observed), the book itself not fetched; the two versions as the field poses them: Shepardson, "Why Are There Two Versions of Meno's Paradox?", The Southern Journal of Philosophy 60(3):465-486, 2022, at its opening as the publisher's page carries it. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the paper's abstract observed at pass one and at the hardness (the paradox as an argument about inquiry into what one does not know; the classical dissolution by partial knowledge leaving the acquisition version), an account of primitive acquisition not presupposing the primitives standing there as what the acquisition version demands. Approaches: the partial-knowledge and recollection routes at that paper and the learning routes at the references at the address, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Partial knowledge — dissolves the classical version, not reaching the concept-acquisition version. Recollection — not taken up as literal by later accounts. Statistical and associative learning — accounts for acquisition of many categories from data, whether the primitives are learned or presupposed being the dispute, the architecture supplying them, which is the objection. Radical concept nativism — concludes primitives are innate, widely resisted, with no generally accepted refutation of the argument itself. Developmental studies — document the sequence of acquisition, establishing when rather than how the recognition problem is solved.

**Narrowing recorded.** Partial knowledge narrows the reach: the account stands for acquisitions specifiable in prior terms. Statistical learning narrows the reach: acquisition stands accounted for where the architecture supplies the primitives.

**Progress.** The dissolution of the classical version. The categories acquired by statistical learners from data. The documented acquisition sequences.

**Common requirement.** That there is a true expected premium the models should reproduce.

**Addresses.** Statement, in substance: the founding paper, Mehra and Prescott, "The equity premium: A puzzle", Journal of Monetary Economics 15(2):145-161, 1985, at its abstract and introduction as the author's page, a university index and a copy carry them verbatim (restrictions that a class of general equilibrium models place upon the average returns of equity and Treasury bills strongly violated by the data over ninety years, the result robust to specification and measurement; for such economies the average real annual yield on equity at most four-tenths of a per cent above short-term debt, in sharp contrast to the six per cent premium observed; the largest premium obtainable in the model thirty-five hundredths of a per cent; an equilibrium model that is not an Arrow-Debreu economy most likely the one to rationalize both), the paper itself not fetched; the Statement standing as it stood. Hardness, and the failure of proposed resolutions along crucial dimensions: Mehra, "The Equity Premium: Why is it a Puzzle?", NBER Working Paper 9512, 2003, abstract. The twenty-year review of explanation classes and the originators' later leaning: Mehra and Prescott, "The equity premium in retrospect", Handbook of the Economics of Finance, 2003. The parametric-restriction counter-argument: Kocherlakota 1996, as reported at arXiv:1909.13019 §1. Disaster calibration to thirty-five countries: Barro 2006, as reported at the same address. Recent European decrease and the standard model's fit: the European-markets analysis reported at ResearchGate 228291610. Survivorship bias: Jorion and Goetzmann 1999, as cited at arXiv:cs/0512027 §D. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding paper's abstract and introduction observed at v342 (the models' restrictions strongly violated by the data; an equilibrium model that is not an Arrow-Debreu economy most likely the one to rationalize both returns), a model reproducing the premium on independently measured parameters standing there as the paper's own closing. Approaches: the risk-aversion and non-standard-model routes at that introduction and the references at the address. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Raising risk aversion — reproduces the premium and produces the risk-free rate puzzle, the same problem at the other price, with the recorded counter-argument that both puzzles follow from the parametric restrictions the original model imposed on the discount factor and the aversion coefficient. Habit formation — a fitted specification, recorded in later work as predicting a value much higher than observed in recent European data. Recursive preferences — separate risk aversion from intertemporal substitution, each introducing parameters not independently measured. Rare disasters — the historical consumption sample recorded as unrepresentative, the originating country having experienced no peacetime peak-to-trough decline of the relevant size while many others did, with calibration to an international dataset of thirty-five countries reproducing the premium under standard preferences and not testable within the sample. Heterogeneous agents with borrowing constraints — the representative-agent assumption relaxed and a fraction of the population constrained from holding the optimal portfolio, generating larger premia and requiring additional structural assumptions about who is constrained and why; recorded as the originators' own later leaning. Behavioural explanations — loss aversion and myopic evaluation, surveyed as a class. Survivorship bias — the surviving-market sample proposed as inflating the estimate. Broadening the sample — cross-country and long-horizon datasets reducing the estimate somewhat, with a substantial decrease in European markets recorded after the recent recession and a standard model then reproducing the premium without extreme aversion.

**Progress.** The measured premium across samples. The reduction under broadened samples. The fits obtained under each specification, with their parameter costs stated.

**Common requirement.** That the reports measure the same thing in both comparisons.

**Unreachability.** Both accounts read the extended series differently, which is the instruments disagreeing, and whether the log form is correct is not settled by data that fits both. The sameness is not preserved across populations and times by responses on bounded scales.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Easterlin, Angelescu McVey, Switek, Sawangfa and Zweig, "The happiness-income paradox revisited", Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences 107(52):22463-22468, 2010, at its abstract as the journal's page carries it verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the paradox stated as the cross-section against the time series: "The Easterlin Paradox", IZA Discussion Paper no. 13923, at its opening as the institute's page carries it, its authors not observed. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the paper's abstract observed at pass one and at the hardness (the paradox holding over the long term and not the short in the authors' reading; the dispute over the series and the measure continuing), a measure demonstrably stable across populations and times standing there as what both sides read the series as lacking. Approaches: the series-extension and functional-form routes at that paper and the references at the address for the other side. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Extending the time series — both accounts doing so and reading the extended series differently, the dispute continuing through successive datasets. Changing the functional form — log income removing the apparent satiation, whether the log form is correct not settled by data that fits both. Distinguishing evaluative from experienced wellbeing — the two measures behaving differently, evaluative tracking income further, refining the question without settling the original comparison. Anchoring vignettes — correct for scale interpretation across cultures and change some cross-country rankings, a genuine advance applied to a subset of datasets. Objective correlates — health, longevity, and time-use measures rising with income, accepted by both accounts and disputed as to whether they are the subject.

**Progress.** The extended series. The measured behaviour of the two wellbeing constructs. The corrected cross-country rankings. The objective correlates.

**Statement.** People make choices that violate expected utility axioms, consistently and on reflection. Are the axioms wrong, or the choices? In the field's own posing, the ambiguity side asks the question its founding paper sets as its first section heading, verbatim: "Are there uncertainties that are not risks?", against the sets of constraints on choice under uncertainty, all more or less equivalent in spirit, which carry the implication that for a rational man every uncertainty can be reduced to a risk; the feeling that people behave as though they assigned numerical probabilities even where the frequencies are unknown is what the constraints formalise.

**Accounts.** The axioms describe rationality and the choices are errors: independence follows from a sure-thing principle most endorse when stated abstractly, a person violating it can be presented with a sequence of trades leaving them worse off by their own lights, and many shown the inconsistency revise. A theory of rational choice answers to what people would endorse on reflection, not to what they do.

Many do not revise on reflection, and the ambiguity pattern reflects a real distinction between risk with known probabilities and ambiguity without them, which the axioms cannot represent. A normative theory that competent reflective agents reject is not obviously the normative one.

**Conserving reach.** All the choice patterns the axioms are asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the axioms state the standard. Observed choices, and reports after being shown the inconsistency, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Some revise and some do not, and the split is stable across studies; and the accommodating theories introduce functions fitted to the data they explain, which their authors state. The standard is not reached over the patterns by observed choices or by post-reflection reports.

**Persistence.** A standard the competent reflective agents who reject the axioms would endorse, or agreement on what settles a normative question empirically.

**Addresses.** Statement, the ambiguity side, and hardness: Ellsberg, "Risk, Ambiguity, and the Savage Axioms", The Quarterly Journal of Economics 75(4):643-669, 1961, at its section headings as the publisher's page carries them verbatim and at its opening and closing passages as a reader's page carries them verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the reply and its counter as the field's history records them: Moscati, "Ellsberg 1961: text, context, influence", at its opening as the repository page carries it, its venue and year not observed; the risk side: Allais, "Le comportement de l'homme rationnel devant le risque: critique des postulats et axiomes de l'ecole americaine", Econometrica 21(4):503-546, 1953, as the society's page carries the citation, the paper's own posing not returned this sitting and keyed at the next pass. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding paper's opening observed at pass one and at the hardness (the deliberate violation of the axioms by reflective agents, the question whether the axioms or the choices should yield), a standard such agents would endorse standing there as the paper's own question. Approaches: the re-asking and independence-weakening routes at that paper and the references at the address for the later theories. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Showing the inconsistency and re-asking — some revising, some not, the split stable across studies. Weakening independence — rank-dependent and prospect theories accommodating the first pattern, each introducing functions fitted to the data they explain, which their authors state. Non-additive beliefs and multiple priors — accommodate the ambiguity pattern by representing ambiguity, establishing representability and not which representation is correct. Raising the stakes — violations persisting at substantial stakes in several studies, the highest-stakes evidence limited. Neuroeconomic measurement — distinct activity for ambiguity and risk, establishing the distinction is registered without adjudicating the normative question.

**Accounts.** Not a single rate: hyperbolic discounting fits the reversals directly and is documented across humans and animals, the reversals following from the shape of the curve rather than from any change in preference.

Measured hyperbolic patterns can arise from aggregation over heterogeneous exponential discounters, from uncertainty about receipt, and from procedural features of the elicitation: individual-level data are less clearly hyperbolic than aggregate data, and the reversals may reflect changing information rather than changing preference.

**Unreachability.** Estimated rates vary by elicitation format and by domain, and field estimates vary by orders of magnitude across studies, which is the instruments disagreeing on the same quantity. The measurement is not preserved across formats and domains by elicited choices.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Frederick, Loewenstein and O'Donoghue, "Time Discounting and Time Preference: A Critical Review", Journal of Economic Literature 40(2):351-401, 2002, at its abstract as the journal's page carries it verbatim and at its passage on unitary time preference as a copy the field's pages carry verbatim, the paper itself not fetched. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the critical review's abstract observed at pass one and at the hardness (the discount rate varying by orders of magnitude across studies and by elicitation, the construct's validity the question), a measurement stable across formats and domains standing there as what the review finds absent. Approaches: the individual-level and format-varying routes at that review's sections, observed at pass one. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Eliciting at individual level — reduces the aggregation artefact, heterogeneity within individuals across domains remaining. Varying elicitation format — changes the estimated rates substantially, documented, and the standing methodological problem. Controlling for uncertainty of receipt — reduces apparent hyperbolicity in some designs, not eliminated. Field measurement from real decisions — estimates from borrowing and saving behaviour varying by orders of magnitude across studies and domains. Commitment device studies — show demand for commitment, supporting the reversal account behaviourally, the interpretation of demand contested.

**Narrowing recorded.** Individual-level elicitation narrows the reach: the measurement stands for an individual within a domain rather than across domains. Controlling for uncertainty narrows the reach: the reduction stands in the designs where it is applied.

**Statement.** Large investments in information technology were followed by measured productivity growth that did not rise correspondingly. Where did the gains go? In the field's own posing, the relationship between information technology and productivity is widely discussed but little understood: delivered computing power in the economy rose by more than two orders of magnitude over the preceding two decades while productivity, especially in the service sector, stagnated, the observation carried in the field's canonical sentence, verbatim: "You can see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics".

**Accounts.** Measurement misses them: output quality, variety, and free digital services are poorly captured in national accounts, the gains being real but unmeasured, with successive studies finding substantial unmeasured consumer surplus.

Mismeasurement is insufficient in magnitude: the slowdown is broad across countries and sectors, including sectors with little digital output, and plausible mismeasurement adjustments do not close the gap. The gains may be smaller than expected, or delayed by complementary reorganisation.

**Conserving relation.** A true productivity change approximated by the statistics, the approximation standing for output the accounts do not price.

**Conserving reach.** Output including what the accounts do not price.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over output including what the accounts do not price. Priced transactions are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Brynjolfsson, "The Productivity Paradox of Information Technology", Communications of the ACM 36(12):66-77, 1993, at its opening as the field's pages carry it verbatim, at its passage on measurement in services as the publisher's page carries it, and at its passage on lags as the working-paper version carries it ("The Productivity Paradox of Information Technology: Review and Assessment", MIT Center for Coordination Science Working Paper 130), the paper itself not fetched; the canonical sentence as a general reference page carries it verbatim, attributed there to Solow, its original place of publication keyed at the next pass. **Accounts.** The transformation can be completed: successive formulations, including single-system interpretations that value inputs and outputs at prices rather than values, preserve the aggregate equalities and remove the inconsistency the original procedure carried.

**Specifications.** There is a magnitude, labour value, standing behind price — local. Aggregate equalities between the two systems should hold — local.

**Unreachability.** The aggregate equalities cannot all be preserved simultaneously in the standard framework, which is proved; and prices and profit are determined without value magnitudes, which is not disputed as mathematics. The determination by value magnitudes is not reached over the prices by observed prices and physical tables.

**Persistence.** A role for value magnitudes in the determination not already discharged by the physical data, or agreement that the equalities are definitional.

**Approaches.** The corrected procedure — the aggregate equalities proved not all simultaneously preservable in the standard framework. Single-system interpretations — preserve the equalities by redefining the value of inputs, their critics holding the redefinition makes the equalities definitional. The physical-determination critique — establishes that prices and profit are determined without value magnitudes, not disputed as mathematics. Empirical correlation studies — high correlation between labour values and prices across sectors, contested as an artefact of sector size, examined and not settled. Rejecting the framework — alternatives determining prices differently, the dispute then being between frameworks rather than within one.

**Narrowing recorded.** Single-system interpretations weaken the relation: the equalities stand under a redefinition of input value, which their critics hold makes them definitional.

**Progress.** The proof that the aggregate equalities cannot all hold. The physical determination result. The measured correlations and the sector-size objection to them.

The identification is not reached over the decisions by choices under nominal presentation.

**Persistence.** A decision setting in which the two accounts predict differently, or a measurement of the decision weight rather than the registration.

**Common requirement.** That the maintenance of sex is accounted for by a benefit exceeding the cost.

**Conserving relation.** A benefit measured against the two-fold cost, the measurement standing across the taxa.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the advantage, measured against a stated two-fold cost. Particular systems where the comparison can be made are what is measured. The two are not the same.

The measurement is not preserved across the taxa by particular systems.

**Persistence.** A benefit measured against the two-fold cost across taxa, or independently measured weights for the combined mechanisms.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Otto, "The Evolutionary Enigma of Sex", The American Naturalist 174(S1):S1-S14, 2009, at its abstract as the journal's page carries it verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the cost as stated and measured, and the systems at Reach: Kobayashi and Hasegawa, "A female-biased sex ratio reduces the twofold cost of sex", Scientific Reports 6:23982, 2016, at its abstract and opening as the archive's page carries them verbatim, the paper itself not fetched; the experimental measurement in a natural system as the field cites it: Gibson, Delph and Lively, "The two-fold cost of sex: experimental evidence from a natural system", Evolution Letters 1(1):6-15, 2017, its abstract not observed. **Approaches.** Systems with mixed reproduction — support the parasite account in those systems, with measured parasite pressure, system-specific. Experimental evolution with and without recombination — recombination speeding adaptation under stress in several organisms, establishing a benefit, its magnitude relative to the cost contested. Mutation accumulation experiments — confirm the ratchet in asexual lines, establishing the mechanism, the rates required for the deterministic account being at the edge of measured values. Ancient asexual lineages — the standing counterexample weakened by subsequent findings of horizontal transfer and other genetic exchange, which complicates the categories. Theoretical pluralism — most current accounts combining mechanisms, fitting, the combination's weights not independently measured.

**Narrowing recorded.** The parasite account narrows the reach: the advantage stands measured in the systems where parasite pressure is measured. Experimental evolution narrows the reach: the benefit stands under stress in the organisms tested.

**Accounts.** Evolved additional suppression: larger species carry additional tumour-suppressor mechanisms — many copies of a key suppressor gene in one lineage, alterations in DNA repair pathways in another — and the suppression scales with the risk.

**Conserving relation.** Lifetime risk compared across species, the comparison standing on standardised incidence.

**Conserving reach.** All species the comparison is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over lifetime risk across species. Necropsy and veterinary records, unevenly sampled across species and conditions, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Comparative genomics establishes mechanisms without quantifying their sufficiency, and multistage models fit with parameters estimated from the same data; the sampling bias is acknowledged in the studies themselves. The comparison is not reached over the species by unevenly sampled records.

**Approaches.** Comparative genomics — identifies suppressor duplications and repair alterations in large species, establishing mechanisms exist, their quantitative sufficiency unmeasured. Zoo and necropsy databases — assemble incidence across many species and confirm weak scaling with body size, sampling bias acknowledged in the studies themselves. Multistage modelling — reproduces patterns under fitted parameters, the parameters not independently measured. Cell-level experiments — cells from large species showing different sensitivity to damage, establishing differences, the link to whole-organism incidence unmeasured. Within-species scaling — larger individuals within a species showing higher risk, consistent with the framing and not resolving the cross-species case.

**Narrowing recorded.** Within-species scaling narrows the reach: the relation stands within a species rather than across species. Cell-level experiments narrow the reach: the differences stand at cell level, the link to organism incidence unmeasured.

**Progress.** The identified suppressor duplications and repair alterations. The confirmed weak scaling with body size. The measured within-species scaling. The cell-level sensitivity differences.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether sequence has a fitness-relevant role. Biochemical activity, conservation, and effects of deletion are what is measured — three criteria giving different answers on the same sequence. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Three criteria of function return different fractions on the same sequence, and the field debated exactly whether activity is function in print. The sorting is not preserved across the instruments by any of the three criteria.

**Accounts.** Equilibrium does not arrive: environmental fluctuation, mixing, and predation reset competition before exclusion completes, and the exclusion principle applies to an equilibrium the system does not reach.

The niches are not being counted correctly: spatial structure, temporal partitioning, and species-specific predation supply more limiting factors than the resource count records, and coexistence is niche-based after all.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all limiting factors operating. Measured resource concentrations and identified predators are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each added measured factor raises the ceiling and whether the count converges is open; and the coexistence framework accommodates both accounts without measuring their weights in natural communities. The count is not reached over the operating factors by measured concentrations and identified predators.

**Addresses.** Statement, in substance: the founding paper, Hutchinson, "The Paradox of the Plankton", American Naturalist 95(882):137-145, 1961, doi 10.1086/282171, at its opening as two indexes and citing reviews carry it verbatim (the problem presented by the phytoplankton essentially how it is possible for a number of species to coexist in a relatively isotropic or unstructured environment all competing for the same sorts of materials; the very paradoxical situation of the plankton of relatively large bodies of water), the paper itself not fetched; a later review at its opening as the publisher's page carries it (the paradox resting on two implicit, perhaps naive postulates, that all plankton species have similar requirements and that the marine environment is relatively homogeneous in space and time), Biology Letters, 2022, doi 10.1098/rsbl.2022.0207, its authors not carried; the Statement standing as it stood. Hardness, the theoretical species counts, and the statement that the mechanisms resolved the paradox and imposed additional problems: the conceptual-issues paper on modern coexistence theory, arXiv:2201.07926, introduction. The failure of the originally proposed mechanism, and its continued citation: Li and Chesson, American Naturalist 188(2), 2016, and the storage-effect analysis at arXiv:1904.10845 §1. Storage effect and buffered growth: arXiv:2201.06691. Time-series selection estimates across 148 series: Fox, Ecology 2010, abstract. Spatial, turbulence, and toxin routes: the plankton-paradox review, ResearchGate 263267841. Trait-structured ocean models and slowed exclusion: ICES Journal of Marine Science 71(2), 2014. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding paper's opening observed at v342 (how a number of species can coexist in a relatively unstructured environment all competing for the same materials; the mechanism proposed, permanent failure to reach equilibrium, the argument recorded as incomplete) and at the later review's two implicit postulates. Approaches: the originally proposed mechanism at that opening and the later routes at the review at the address. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** The originally proposed mechanism — coexistence by permanent failure to reach equilibrium as external factors change; the argument recorded as incomplete, with no model validating it, and the specific mechanism shown by later modelling to fail, while continuing to be cited. The coexistence framework — partitions mechanisms into stabilising and equalising, and into fluctuation-dependent and fluctuation-independent, each measurable in principle. The storage effect — species specialising on different aspects of a variable environment, with buffered growth when not actively taking up resources, supporting an arbitrary number of species in theory and tested in the field in specific systems. Relative nonlinearity — species specialising on resource variance rather than mean level, the variation generated externally by seasonal turnover or internally by consumer-resource dynamics, supporting a number growing quadratically with the resources. Chaotic dynamics — competition among few resources generating chaos permitting many species, established in models. Spatial and self-generated heterogeneity — segregation, mesoscale turbulence and coherent vortices, and toxin production, each proposed as a distinct route. Neutral and equalising accounts — diversity patterns reproduced with no niche differences, and trait-structured ocean models in which exclusion remains an intrinsic property but its rate is slowed. Time-series estimation of selection — net and fluctuating selection estimated across ninety-nine annual zooplankton series and forty-nine algal series, with within-year net selection found statistically significant and ecologically weak. Adding measured factors — each addition raising the ceiling, whether the count converges being the open part. Long-term microcosm experiments — sustained coexistence observed beyond the resource count.

**Narrowing recorded.** Chaotic dynamics narrows the reach: sufficiency stands established in models. Microcosm experiments narrow the reach: the coexistence stands observed under laboratory conditions.

Synchrony is a correlate, and no mechanism pattern-matching it has been identified: hierarchical convergence onto neurons responsive to conjunctions, plus attention, accounts for the phenomena without a separate binding process, and the problem may be posed by an assumption about how experience must be constructed.

**Conserving relation.** A combining operation identified, the identification standing as cause rather than correlate.

**Conserving reach.** All the cases unity is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what makes the features one object in experience. Neural activity and behavioural report are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Synchrony is documented and whether it is causal or a consequence of shared drive is where the field's analyses disagree, with the disputes in print on both sides; and no mechanism pattern-matching the synchrony has been identified. The identification is not reached over the cases by neural activity and behavioural report.

**Persistence.** A mechanism pattern-matching the synchrony identified, or a manipulation separating cause from consequence across the cases.

Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the two sources at the address, observed at pass one and at the hardness (the problem of how separately represented features are combined; the candidate mechanisms, synchrony among them, their causal standing disputed), a mechanism pattern-matching the synchrony or a manipulation parting cause from consequence standing there as what the disputes in print ask. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Gamma synchrony studies — documented across species and tasks, whether causal or a consequence of shared drive disputed, the disputes in print on both sides. Illusory conjunction studies — binding failures under attentional load, establishing attention's role, not a mechanism. Convergence and conjunction cells — found in higher visual areas, handling some conjunctions, combinatorial coverage being the objection. Optogenetic manipulation of synchrony — behavioural effects in specific paradigms, the strongest causal route, scope so far narrow. Dissolving the problem — unity argued to be a feature of report rather than a construction requiring a process, reframing, the phenomena remaining to be accounted.

**Narrowing recorded.** Optogenetic manipulation narrows the reach: the causal result stands for the specific paradigms manipulated. Conjunction cells narrow the reach: the account stands for the conjunctions convergence covers.

**Progress.** The documented gamma coherence across species and tasks. The measured binding failures under attentional load. The identified conjunction cells. The behavioural effects of synchrony manipulation.

**Conserving relation.** A primary function identified, the identification standing separately from the state that carries it.

**Unreachability.** Deprivation produces cognitive and physiological deficits together, which both accounts predict, and the clearance results have been contested in technique, which is the instruments in dispute. The identification is not reached over the animals by correlates and deprivation effects.
