Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Narrowing recorded.** Replay studies narrow the reach: the informational role stands established in the structures where replay is documented. Clearance measurement narrows the reach: the result stands in the species and technique measured.

**Statement.** I know my own experience directly. I infer everyone else's from behaviour. What justifies the inference?

**Accounts.** Inference to the best explanation: other bodies resemble mine, behave as mine does, and share my physiology, and experience in them is by far the best explanation of the behaviour.

The inference is from a single case, which is the weakest form of induction there is, and no independent check on it is available: alternatives — that experience is a way of describing behaviour, or that the demand for justification is malformed — have been developed precisely because the argument from analogy is regarded as insufficient.

**Specifications.** Experience is directly known in one's own case and not in others' — local. The asymmetry is a fact about access rather than about the concepts — local.

**Common requirement.** That there is something in others whose presence the inference is about.

**Conserving relation.** A justification of the inference, standing on a check independent of the single case.

**Conserving reach.** All others the inference is made about.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over experience in another. Behaviour and physiology are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The inference is from one case with no check, formally weak by a result both accounts accept; inference to the best explanation depends on the framework already admitting experience as an explanans, which is what is in question; and criterial accounts remove the inference and are contested as changing the question. The justification is not reached over the others by behaviour and physiology.

**Persistence.** A check on the inference independent of the single case, or agreement that no justification is owed.

**Addresses.** Statement, the epistemological and the conceptual problem parted: Avramides, "Other Minds", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy. Hardness: the same entry, at passages of its text as the encyclopedia's page and the Fall 2025 edition carry them verbatim (any knowledge of other minds, or any justification for belief in them, held by the defenders of the argument from analogy and of best explanation alike to be indirect; a thin sceptical problem of everyday life parted from a thick, distinctively philosophical one leading to the possibility of solipsism; thinking of mental states as lying behind and causing behaviour possibly breaking a deep connection between mentality and behaviour), the entry itself not fetched, its section numbers and revision date not carried at the returns; the archived editions before 2019 carry a different text, whose passages on the conceptual problem (how a concept of mental states other than one's own is possible; the same materials producing both problems; some holding the conceptual problem the difficult one) are observed here verbatim, their author not carried at the returns. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the same entry, at the passages observed at pass two (any knowledge of other minds or justification for belief in them held by the defenders of the argument from analogy to rest on the single case; the criterial alternative removing the inference), a check independent of the single case standing there as what the argument's defenders acknowledge it lacks. Approaches: the analogy and criterial routes at the same entry's sections, observed at pass two. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Argument from analogy — one case, no check, its weakness acknowledged by its defenders. Criterial accounts — behaviour in context as a criterion rather than evidence, removing the inference and contested as changing the question. Inference to the best explanation — depends on the explanatory framework already admitting experience as an explanans, which is what is in question. Neurophysiological similarity — establishes similarity, which is the analogy premise measured. Denying the asymmetry — self-knowledge held also to be inferential, levelling the two cases without supplying the missing check.

**Narrowing recorded.** Criterial accounts weaken the relation: behaviour in context stands as a criterion in place of an inference to be justified.

**Conserving relation.** Content fixed by a relation between system and world, the fixing standing against alternative assignments.

**Conserving reach.** All symbols the content relation is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the content relation. The system's behaviour and its causal history are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Indicator theories face the disjunction problem and misrepresentation cases, documented and unresolved; and content fixed by selection history inherits indeterminacy of the selected function. The fixing is not reached over the symbols by behaviour and causal history.

**Persistence.** A relation fixing content against the alternative assignments, or agreement that content is not fixed by such a relation.

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness, and the room argument and the candidate solution at Approaches: Harnad, "The Symbol Grounding Problem", Physica D 42(1-3):335-346, 1990, at its abstract as the publisher's page and the repository copy carry it verbatim and at its closing passages as the repository copy carries them, the paper itself not fetched; the earlier form of the problem as the paper cites it: Searle, 1980. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the founding paper's abstract observed at pass one and at the hardness (how the meanings of the symbols in a system can be made intrinsic rather than parasitic on interpretation; the candidate solution grounding elementary symbols in categorical representations), a relation fixing content against the alternative assignments standing there as the paper's own question. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Robotic grounding — builds systems whose categories arise from interaction, establishing categories are acquirable, whether they are about anything being the original question. Causal and informational semantics — asymmetric dependence and indicator theories, each facing the disjunction problem and misrepresentation cases, documented and unresolved. Teleosemantics — fixes content by selection history, facing indeterminacy of the selected function, contested. The room argument — holds syntax insufficient for semantics, the systems reply and the robot reply standing as responses, the exchange not converged. Large language models — acquire behaviour previously thought to require grounding, what this shows about content actively disputed.

**Narrowing recorded.** Teleosemantics narrows the reach: content stands fixed by selection history where a function was selected. Robotic grounding narrows the reach: categories stand acquired through interaction in the systems built.

**Progress.** The acquirable categories in built systems. The documented disjunction and misrepresentation cases. The behaviour acquired by models without sensorimotor interaction.

**Accounts.** Yes, increasingly: circuits have been identified, features localised, and specific behaviours traced to specific components, sparse decomposition methods extract features more interpretable than raw units, and the methods improve steadily.

Explanations obtained so far cover a small fraction of computation, and the standard for success is not agreed: explanations that predict behaviour under intervention are rare, many are post hoc narratives fitting observed activity, and the field's own evaluations find explanations that appear plausible and fail causal tests.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the system's computation is. Activations, ablations, and behaviour under intervention are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Probing was shown by the field to recover information the system does not use, a proved limitation on its own instrument; and causal results are component-specific and do not compose into whole-model accounts. The explanation is not reached over the whole computation by activations, ablations, and intervention.

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness and the standard at Persistence: Lipton, "The Mythos of Model Interpretability", arXiv:1606.03490, 2016, and Communications of the ACM 61(10):36-43, 2018, at its abstract as the archive's and the journal's pages carry it verbatim, the paper itself not fetched. Approaches: the visualisation, probing and ablation routes at the founding paper's abstract observed at pass one, in part, their endings at the references at the address for the controls the field established. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Feature visualisation and probing — produces plausible descriptions, probes recovering information the system does not use, which the field established and now controls for. Causal ablation and patching — stronger, results component-specific and not composing into whole-model accounts. Sparse dictionary methods — extract more interpretable features than raw units, coverage of total computation partial and measured. Formal verification — available for small networks and specific properties, scaling poorly, which is the known limit. Behavioural evaluation — establishes what the system does, not how.

**Narrowing recorded.** Formal verification narrows the reach: the guarantee stands for small networks and specific properties. Causal ablation narrows the reach: the results stand component by component. Sparse dictionary methods narrow the reach: coverage stands measured and partial.

**Progress.** The identified circuits and localised features. The measured coverage of sparse decompositions. The established limitation on probing. The verified properties of small networks.

**Accounts.** By identifying the mechanisms that produce it in the case we know: behavioural and neural markers — nociception with motivational trade-offs, flexible learning, integration — provide graded evidence, and precautionary frameworks are built on them for animals.

The markers are selected by resemblance to us and have no independent validation: a system engineered to display them displays them, an organism with different architecture may have experience without them, and no test distinguishes presence from absence.

**Conserving relation.** Markers bearing on presence, the bearing standing on validation independent of resemblance.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over presence, indexed by markers with no independent validation. Behaviour, architecture, and physiology are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The marker frameworks lack independent validation, stated by their own authors, and an engineered system can produce the same behavioural pattern; theory-driven tests apply a theory that is what is in dispute. The bearing is not reached over the systems by behaviour, architecture, and physiology.

**Addresses.** Statement and hardness: Birch, The Edge of Sentience: Risk and Precaution in Humans, Other Animals, and AI, Oxford University Press, 2024, doi:10.1093/9780191966729.001.0001, at its abstract as the publisher's pages carry it verbatim, and at its definition of sentience (p. 1) and its precautionary principle (p. 18) as the field's review carries them (Leonardo Reviews, 2024), the book itself not fetched; the marker frameworks at Approaches as the field cites them: Birch, Burn, Schnell, Browning and Crump, Review of the Evidence of Sentience in Cephalopod Molluscs and Decapod Crustaceans, 2021; Birch, Schnell and Clayton, "Dimensions of animal consciousness", Trends in Cognitive Sciences 24(10):789-801, 2020. Persistence, no settling: the closing condition at the book's abstract observed at pass one and at the hardness (the framework of markers and precaution for sentience candidates; the validation problem stated within the frameworks), a marker validated independently of resemblance to the case we know standing there as the framework's own stated difficulty. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Approaches.** Marker frameworks — applied to invertebrates with legislative consequence in several jurisdictions, establishing a graded evidential practice, the validation problem stated within the frameworks themselves. Comparative neuroanatomy — finds candidate structures in distant lineages, establishing analogous organisation, the inference to experience being the original question. Behavioural trade-off tests — motivational trade-offs against noxious stimuli demonstrated in decapods and insects, the strongest behavioural evidence, engineered systems producing the same pattern. Theory-driven tests — apply a theory of consciousness and read off a verdict, the theory being what is in dispute. Denying determinacy — no fact in marginal cases, contested, and consequential for practice.

**Narrowing recorded.** Marker frameworks narrow the reach: graded evidence stands for the taxa the markers were developed against.

**Accounts.** Statistical assignment works: temporal difference methods propagate value backward without a model, eligibility traces distribute credit across a decaying window, and backpropagation through time assigns it exactly in differentiable settings. These are the basis of systems that learn tasks with horizons of thousands of steps from sparse reward, and the convergence properties of the methods are proved for stated conditions.

These assign credit relative to a model and a reward specification, both supplied: where the sequence is long, the signal sparse, and the environment non-stationary, the assignment is unidentifiable in principle — many action sequences being consistent with the same outcome — and the methods succeed by imposing structure rather than by recovering it.

**Conserving relation.** Contribution of individual actions identified, the identification standing without a model or reward specification supplied.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over contribution of individual actions. Outcomes, and counterfactual rollouts within a model, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Formal conditions for identifying effects from observational data are proved and are frequently unmet in sequential settings, and reward shaping changes what is credited. The identification is not reached over the sequences by outcomes and within-model rollouts.

**Statement.** An object has properties. Is there something that has them, or is the object just the properties together? In the field's own posing, the issue is whether substances are in some sense reducible to their properties or whether there exists some further component, a substratum, both views having been defended; the bundle theorist has two options, substances as no more than bundles of properties conceived as universals, or as no more than bundles of property-instances, tropes, and on either the claim that substances are no more than bundles may be regarded as a conceptual analysis of the concept of substance or as a de facto but not a conceptual truth.

**Accounts.** Selection and construction: mathematics is developed in continual contact with physical problems, the branches that apply were often built for application, and the successes are salient while the vast unapplied remainder is not counted.

The selection account does not cover the striking cases: non-Euclidean geometry, group theory, and complex analysis were developed with no physical application in view and later proved indispensable, and predictive success far beyond the data used to construct the theory is not accounted for by contact or selection.

**Common requirement.** That the applicability is a phenomenon with an explanation.

**Persistence.** An account covering the striking cases that both accounts accept, or agreement on what the historical record shows.

**Statement.** Mixing two different gases raises entropy. Mixing a gas with itself does not. The change is discontinuous as the gases are made more alike. Why? In the field's own posing, the paradox is a set of open questions as to how sameness of gases or fluids is to be treated in thermodynamics and statistical mechanics, stated as three puzzles: the discontinuity puzzle, why the entropy of mixing is independent of the degree of similarity of the gases and zero only when they are the same; the extensivity puzzle, how an extensive entropy function can be defined in classical statistical mechanics; and the microrealism puzzle, how there can fail to be an entropy of mixing even for samples of the same gas, given that the particles undergo much the same microscopic motions on mixing whether exactly alike or only similar.

**Accounts.** Indistinguishability is fundamental: identical particles are not merely similar but the same kind, the correct counting divides by the permutations, quantum mechanics supplies this, and the discontinuity is real because identity is not a matter of degree.

The entropy of mixing depends on what can be separated by available operations, so it is relative to the capacity to discriminate: the paradox arises from treating entropy as a property of the system rather than of the description, and the discontinuity is a discontinuity in the description.

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about the entropy change on mixing.

**Conserving relation.** Entropy determined as a property of the system, the determination standing independently of the operations available.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether entropy is a property of the system or of the description. Measurable work extractable given available operations is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The information-theoretic treatment reproduces every measurement and relocates entropy to the description; the resource-theoretic formulation states the relativity rather than removing it; and particles continuously interpolating between two species are not available, so the discontinuity is not directly probed. The determination is not reached over the mixings by extractable work.

**Persistence.** A measurement of entropy independent of the operations available, or a direct probe of the interpolating case.

**Statement.** An examination will be held next week and its day will be a surprise. The students argue it cannot happen. It happens, and is a surprise. Where is the error?

**Accounts.** The backward induction is invalid: the announcement's truth conditions involve the students' knowledge, and after the argument they no longer know the exam will occur, so the eliminating step fails at the first move.

**Specifications.** The announcement is known to be true — local. Knowledge persists through the reasoning — local. Surprise is definable in terms of what is known — local.

**Unreachability.** Each proposed solution has been met by a reformulation restoring the puzzle, documented over seventy years. The location is not preserved across the formulations by any reconstruction.

**Persistence.** A location of the error standing against the reformulations, or agreement on which assumption the reformulations must drop.

**Statement.** Could there be a being physically identical to a person with no experience at all? If it is conceivable, what follows? The field's own name for the problem the argument is posed within is the hard problem of consciousness, and its canonical statement stands verbatim: why is the performance of these functions accompanied by experience?

Conceivability does not entail possibility: water without H₂O is conceivable and impossible, the a posteriori necessity cases show the inference fails in exactly the structurally similar cases, and the conceivability may reflect a limitation in how the physical is represented rather than a metaphysical fact.

**Common requirement.** That the physical facts either fix the experiential ones or do not.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the physical facts fix the experiential ones, standing on what is examinable.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a modal relation between two sets of facts. Actual systems are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The two-dimensional framework and the phenomenal concept strategy are technical and unresolved, the strategy facing a dilemma stated precisely by both accounts: either it fails to account for the concepts physically or it concedes the point. The determination is not reached over the modal relation by actual systems.

**Statement.** Why the probability that a taxon goes extinct is approximately constant over its lifetime, independent of how long it has already survived — the law read from fossil survivorship curves — and whether the constancy is produced by continual coevolution among interacting species, each improvement met by another's, so that adapting leaves relative standing as it was. The hypothesis is carried by the field separately as an account of the maintenance of sexual reproduction.

**Statement.** Anatomically modern humans appear long before the changes in behaviour and settlement usually taken as their signature. Why the gap? In the field's own posing, the human genome, and hence the human brain at birth, may not have changed greatly over the past sixty thousand years, yet many of the major behavioural changes associated with most human societies are very much more recent, some appearing with the sedentary revolution of some ten thousand years ago, among them activities implying the emergence of powerful concepts of value and of the sacred; the paradox asks why, if the biological basis of the species has been established for perhaps as much as two hundred thousand years, the novel behavioural aspects of its sapient status took so long to emerge, all major evidence in the archaeological record of important changes in intelligent behaviour coming long after the appearance of modern anatomy.

**Approaches.** Computation — verified past 4 × 10¹⁸, returning more of the same domain. The circle method — proves the ternary case by estimating the relevant exponential sums, the binary case beyond its reach for reasons the method makes precise. Sieve methods — every sufficiently large even number a prime plus a number with at most two prime factors, recorded as the quintessential exhibition of the parity barrier, sieve methods counting numbers with few prime factors more readily than numbers with exactly one. Bilinear form estimates — the ternary resolution introduced the idea that estimating certain bilinear forms can break the barrier, with further instances recorded since; the binary case is not among them. Parity-sensitive sieves — developed from the late 1990s and recorded as making the obstruction less of an obstacle, without removing it here. Bounded-error variants — an argument recorded that the obstruction rules out modifications of the bounded-gaps method for the binary case, with the stated escapes being a failure of the underlying randomness principle or control of combinations of more than one multiplicative function. Density and heuristic arguments — predict many representations, not proofs, and known to be unable to become proofs by refinement. Assuming the Riemann hypothesis — improves bounds and does not close the binary case.

**Statement.** A physical account of a process states which physical facts obtain, and it leaves open why those facts are accompanied by experience of the character they are accompanied by. Is the gap in the explanation, or in what the explanation is about?

**Accounts.** The gap is epistemic: the facts are physical and an account of them will close the gap, or the gap follows from how the physical is represented and not from what obtains.

The gap is ontological: an explanatory residue standing where identities elsewhere leave none is evidence that the facts are not exhausted by the physical.

The gap is permanent and neither: the terms available to a self do not couple in the way an explanation of this kind would require, and no account either closes it or licenses a conclusion about what exists.

**Specifications.** An adequate explanation of an identity leaves no residue — local. The residue is a property of the explanation and not of the explainer — local.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the residue bears on what exists, standing on what an explanation delivers.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what an explanation delivers. Explanations given, and reports of what they leave open, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A residue observed at an explanation is observed by a self carrying the concepts the explanation is given in, and no procedure separates a residue in the account from a residue in the observing. The determination is not reached over all accounts by explanations given.

**Persistence.** An account of a physical process from which the experiential character follows in the way the character of water follows from its account, agreed by both.

**Statement.** What theory gives a quantum account of gravitation and recovers general relativity and quantum field theory in the domains where each is established?

**Specifications.** Quantum mechanics applies to the gravitational field — universal under quantum theory. The classical spacetime dynamics are recovered at low curvature — universal under general relativity. Established particle-physics predictions are recovered at accessible energies — universal under quantum field theory.

**Conserving relation.** The account returns one probability assignment for every physical process in its domain and the assignment reduces to the established prediction in each tested limit.

**Reach.** The statement ranges over a joint quantum-gravitational domain. Present experiments test the low-energy limits and indirect consequences of candidate frameworks. The two are not the same: the characteristic scale is so far out of the range of experiment that direct tests have long seemed impossible, the proposals resting on very small deviations from otherwise exact symmetries or on collective effects integrated over long distances, no single experimental approach can directly probe the quantum-gravitational regime while numerous models give different subtle modifications to low-energy physics, and recovering the classical theory at low energies is a consistency requirement rather than a prediction to be tested.

**Persistence.** A mathematically complete account with a discriminating empirical result and the two established limiting theories recovered.

Emergent and holographic programs — gravity reconstructed in restricted settings, with general physical reach open.

**Narrowing recorded.** Semiclassical gravity and effective field theory narrow the reach to regimes below the quantum-gravity scale and return controlled predictions there.

**Statement.** Why is the primordial lithium-7 abundance inferred from old metal-poor stars lower than the abundance predicted by standard big-bang nucleosynthesis using the independently measured baryon density?

**Accounts.** The stellar atmospheres do not preserve the primordial abundance because lithium is depleted or transported.

The nuclear reaction network or its measured rates omit an effect that lowers the predicted abundance.

Physics beyond the standard cosmological model alters nucleosynthesis or destroys lithium afterward.

The abundance inference carries an unrecognized systematic offset.

**Specifications.** The baryon density inferred from the cosmic microwave background applies during nucleosynthesis — universal under standard cosmology. The stellar plateau preserves or permits reconstruction of the primordial abundance — local. The standard nuclear network contains the material production and destruction channels — local.

**Common requirement.** One primordial lithium abundance consistent with nucleosynthesis, the baryon density and the observed light-element record.

**Conserving reach.** Lithium-7 together with the other primordial light nuclides and the independently measured baryon density.

**Reach.** The statement ranges over primordial lithium. The direct record consists of present stellar spectra and cosmological and nuclear inputs from which the primordial value is inferred. **Approaches.** Equal-weight conciliation — respects symmetry and risks excessive skepticism or self-defeat. Steadfast views — retain first-order warrant and risk arbitrary self-favoring. Total-evidence views — weigh both orders and require a non-question-begging reliability assessment. Permissivism — admits multiple rational responses and shifts the problem to the limits of permission.

**Approaches.** Multigroup confirmatory factor analysis — tests nested equality conditions with fit and sample-size sensitivity. Item-response differential-functioning analysis — localizes item differences under an anchor set. Partial invariance — frees selected parameters and conditions inference on the remaining anchors. Approximate and alignment methods — estimate small deviations at the cost of new priors or tolerances.
